TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用横向梯度多层膜镜的软X射线谱偏振仪概念验证方案，并在两个能量范围内测试了不同材料的多层膜反射特性，验证了其对入射偏振角的响应调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展可用于软X射线波段的高灵敏度、高分辨率谱偏振测量技术，以支持天体物理和等离子体诊断等应用。

Method: 构建基于光栅光谱仪与横向梯度多层膜镜的软X射线谱偏振仪原型；测试Cr/Sc（200–400 eV，布儒斯特角）和W/B₄C（500–850 eV，30°入射）两种多层膜在单色偏振X射线下的反射响应。

Result: 观测到反射信号随入射偏振角呈预期调制，证实了该谱偏振仪原理的可行性。

Conclusion: 横向梯度多层膜镜可有效实现软X射线波段的偏振敏感光谱测量，为未来空间或实验室偏振光谱仪器提供了可行技术路径。

Abstract: This work describes proof of concept measurements for a soft X-ray spectropolarimeter that utilizes laterally graded multilayer mirrors within a grating spectrometer. The work tests two multilayers: a Chromium-Scandium multilayer designed to be utilized at Brewster's angle in the 200-400 eV range and a Tungsten Boron Carbide mirror designed to reflect X-rays at 30 degrees incidence in the 500-850 eV range. We present measurements of monochromatic, polarized light reflecting off of these multilayers over a range of polarization angles, demonstrating the expected modulation of signal with respect to incident polarization angle

</details>


### [101] [PhySR: Physics-Informed Neural Network for Super-Resolution Reconstruction in Radio Synthesis Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27750)
*Hongkun Yang,Li Zhang,Ming Zhang,Yong-Feng Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PhySR的物理信息神经网络，用于射电望远镜图像超分辨率重建，通过融合U-Net、动态级联上采样、多尺度特征残差模块与可微分物理前向模型，在无高分辨标签情况下直接从低分辨脏图重建高分辨图像，并在SKA-Mid模拟数据上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射电望远镜阵列受限于天线数量和基线分布，导致空间频率采样不全、成像分辨率受限，并因主波束与合成波束耦合引发模糊、畸变及小尺度结构丢失；现有模型驱动方法逐序去除观测效应易累积误差，且无法直接提升分辨率；数据驱动方法缺乏显式物理约束。

Method: 提出端到端物理信息神经网络PhySR，结合U-Net骨干网络、动态级联上采样、多尺度特征残差模块，以及包含主波束响应、PSF卷积和尺度映射的可微分物理前向模型，实现从低分辨脏图到高分辨图像的直接重建，无需高分辨标签并保持观测域一致性。

Result: 在SKA-Mid模拟数据上，4倍超分辨率下PSNR达44.65 dB、SSIM为0.9940、RMSE为0.0065；相比通用方法，PSNR和SSIM分别提升约13.23 dB和0.3760；相比主流深度学习模型，PSNR和SSIM分别提升6.50 dB和0.0682，RMSE降低0.0069；在2x和8x超分辨率下亦保持稳定，并具有低观测域一致性误差。

Conclusion: PhySR有效解决了射电图像中耦合效应去除、小尺度结构恢复与物理一致性保持三重挑战，兼具高重建精度与强泛化能力，为天文图像超分辨率提供了新范式。

Abstract: Radio telescope arrays are constrained by the number of antennas and baseline distribution, resulting in incomplete spatial-frequency sampling, limited image resolution, and blurring, distortion, and loss of small-scale structures caused by coupling between the primary and synthesized beams. Existing general-purpose model-driven methods remove observational effects sequentially and may accumulate errors, but cannot directly address limited imaging resolution, while data-driven methods lack explicit physical constraints. We propose PhySR, an end-to-end physics-informed neural network that combines a U-Net backbone, dynamic cascaded upsampling, a multiscale feature residual module, and a differentiable physical forward model incorporating the primary beam response, PSF convolution, and scale mapping. PhySR directly reconstructs high-resolution images from low-resolution dirty images without high-resolution labels while maintaining observation-domain consistency. Experiments on simulated SKA-Mid data show that, for 4x super-resolution, PhySR achieves a PSNR of 44.65 dB, an SSIM of 0.9940, and an RMSE of 0.0065. Compared with existing general-purpose methods, PSNR and SSIM improve by approximately 13.23 dB and 0.3760, respectively. Compared with mainstream deep learning models, PSNR and SSIM improve by 6.50 dB and 0.0682, while RMSE decreases by 0.0069. PhySR also remains stable for 2x and 8x super-resolution and achieves low observation-domain consistency errors, demonstrating advantages in coupling-effect removal, small-scale structure recovery, and physical consistency.

</details>


### [102] [DM/WFS mis-registration tracking: Implementation and on-sky validation of SPRINT at LBT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27941)
*Ben Buky,Cédric Taïssir Héritier,Fabio Rossi,Juan Carlos Guerra,Charlotte Z. Bond,Noah Schwartz,Guido Agapito,Enrico Pinna,Sam Ragland,Jean-François Sauvage*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SPRINT方法在大型双筒望远镜（LBT）实时控制系统中的实现与实验验证，用于跟踪和校正变形镜（DM）与波前传感器（WFS）之间的动态失调（旋转、平移、缩放），以维持自适应光学系统稳定性与衍射极限性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型望远镜中变形镜与波前传感器空间分离大，运行中相对失调持续变化，若不实时跟踪校正，将导致AO闭环不稳定，无法达到衍射极限性能。

Method: 将SPRINT算法部署至LBT实时计算机系统，并开展日间实验室测试与实测天文观测验证其对旋转、平移及缩放失调的在线估计与补偿能力。

Result: 成功在LBT上实现SPRINT实时运行，日间与实测数据均证实其能准确、鲁棒地恢复各类失调参数，满足AO系统稳定运行需求。

Conclusion: SPRINT被验证为适用于LBT的可行解决方案，为其AO系统提供关键失调跟踪能力，也为ELT等下一代望远镜相关仪器提供了技术支撑与实践参考。

Abstract: The advent of telescopes with an integrated deformable mirror (DM) presents new challenges for adaptive optics (AO) systems. The alignment between the DM and wavefront sensor (WFS) is expected to regularly evolve during operations due to their large separation. Without tracking and correction, these mis-registrations between the DM and WFS lead to loop instability, preventing diffraction limited performance from being realised. SPRINT\cite{heritier2021} provides an approach to track these mis-registrations during observations. Rotation, shift, and magnification mis-registrations can all be recovered. The Large Binocular Telescope (LBT) currently lacks an operational solution for tracking these mis-registrations, while SPRINT has been selected as the baseline approach for several instruments on the forthcoming Extremely Large Telescope (ELT). We report on the implementation of SPRINT into the LBT real time computer and present experimental results from both daytime and on-sky testing to validate the method.

</details>


### [103] [Focal-Plane Diagnostics of Atmospheric and Dome-Induced Turbulence: Exploring techniques and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28092)
*Begoña García-Lorenzo,Seifeldin Abouelella,Kabir Baig,Zenaida García Rodríguez,Kshitij Ghimire,Moisés Pulido Torres,Alejandro Trujillo Ramos,Jose A. Acosta-Pulido,Julio A. Castro-Almazán,Donaji Esparza-Arredondo,Antonio Eff-Darwich*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出利用标准观测数据（尤其是积分场光谱数据）诊断大气湍流外尺度和圆顶湍流的新方法，发展了基于大气统计的数据驱动三维点扩散函数模型，提升了图像质量预测与建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气湍流（尤其是外尺度LO和圆顶湍流）对地面天文成像质量影响显著，但其常规监测稀缺，限制了PSF建模与图像质量预测精度。

Method: 基于面向视宁度受限积分场光谱（IFS）数据的最新方法，通过分析波长相关的空间轮廓，拓展至其他视宁度受限仪器；并构建数据驱动的三维PSF模型。

Result: 证实常规科学观测（含归档数据）可有效约束关键湍流参数及圆顶湍流的光谱行为，补充专用台址测试设备。

Conclusion: 所提方法为PSF建模、图像重建、图像质量预测及未来极大望远镜（ELT）运行优化提供了实用、可观测的湍流诊断工具。

Abstract: Atmospheric turbulence remains the dominant source of image degradation in ground-based astronomy. Its impact depends not only on the integrated turbulence strength but also on the wavefront spatial-coherence outer scale (\LO) and on turbulence generated within the telescope enclosure (dome seeing). While seeing, coherence time, and related parameters are routinely monitored at major observatories, measurements of \LO\ and dome-induced turbulence remain sparse, limiting our ability to predict image quality and to construct accurate point-spread function (PSF) models. Building on methodology recently developed for seeing-limited integral-field spectroscopic (IFS) data, we present a series of complementary studies showing how spatial information encoded in standard observations can be used to diagnose both atmospheric and dome turbulence. We investigate the feasibility of extending these techniques beyond IFS observations to other seeing-limited instruments through the analysis of wavelength-dependent spatial profiles. We also present the development of data-driven three-dimensional PSF models based on atmospheric statistics for the reconstruction of seeing-limited IFS observations. These results show that widely available seeing-limited scientific observations, both from archival data and routine observatory operations, can provide meaningful and operationally useful constraints on key turbulence parameters and on the spectral behavior of dome seeing, complementing dedicated site-testing instrumentation. The resulting turbulence diagnostics have direct applications to PSF modelling, image reconstruction, image-quality prediction, and the optimization of future ELT operations.

</details>


### [104] [Exploring generative design AI tools for astronomical instrumentation: a CubeSat chassis case study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28217)
*Younes Chahid,Tassos Aretos,Will Cochrane,Katherine Morris,David Isherwood,Zeshan Ali,Graham Wilks,Noah Schwartz,Anmol Goyal,Marcell Westsik,Alastair Macleod,Steve Watson,Anjali Bhatt,David Lunney,Bradley Frank*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种基于AI和有限元分析（FEA）的生成式设计工具在ADOT立方星任务底盘设计中的实际应用，展示了其在多约束机械结构早期概念设计阶段的优势与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械工程CAD中生成式AI工具 adoption受限，而天文仪器机械设计又面临日益增长的热、振动及力学复杂性与紧迫工期，亟需高效设计方法。

Method: 采用AI与FEA结合的生成式设计工具，应用于ADOT CubeSat底盘设计，系统梳理设计、制造与目标约束设定等流程步骤。

Result: 验证了该工具在早期多约束结构概念设计阶段的显著优势，但也暴露其‘黑箱’特性、结果非制造就绪、设置耗时等明显局限。

Conclusion: 生成式AI设计工具对高价值、高复杂度机械部件具有实用潜力，但需克服可解释性、制造集成与易用性等关键障碍才能在机械CAD中广泛应用。

Abstract: Generative design artificial intelligence (AI) tools are currently used in multiple scientific fields, yet their adoption in mechanical engineering computer-aided design (CAD) remains limited due to a lack of disseminated case studies, limited availability of accessible tools, insufficient training in CAD data, the absence of universal editable file formats and more. Mechanical design for astronomical instrumentation faces increasing complexity in thermal, vibrational, and mechanical requirements alongside tight project deadlines. This paper presents a practical evaluation of an AI and FEA based generative design tool applied to chassis design for the Active Deployable Optical Telescope (ADOT) CubeSat mission. Our analysis showcases the workflow steps including the setting of design, manufacturing and objective constraints. This study also shows the clear benefits of these types of tools, especially in the early brainstorming stages of multi-constrained mechanical structures, while also highlighting clear limitations like their black-box nature, the non-manufacturing-ready state of the results, and the time-consuming setup, limiting the tangible gain of these tools to high-value mechanical components.

</details>


### [105] [Segment-level thermal sensitivity analysis for exo-Earth coronagraphy with segmented space telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28393)
*Ananya Sahoo,Laurent Pueyo,Iva Laginja,Bryony F. Nickson,Leonid Pogorelyuk,Laura E. Coyle,Rémi Soummer,Matthew East*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文针对地外类地行星直接成像需求，提出了分段式空间望远镜的镜片面级热稳定性误差预算，明确了实现100 ppt星光抑制所需的超稳望远镜热控要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接成像和表征类地行星需要极高的波前稳定性（星光抑制达100 ppt），而望远镜热漂移会破坏该稳定性，因此需建立分段式主镜的热稳定性误差预算。

Method: 结合STScI开发的多种分段主镜构型与对应离轴~6米口径空间望远镜的孔径调制方案，利用L3Harris提供的精细有限元模型，将主镜位置温度梯度与各子镜波前变化关联；采用基于配对的解析灵敏度模型（静态）与批估计算法（动态）分别分配热容差。

Result: 发现因孔径调制导致各子镜热容差呈非均匀分布，且子镜数量增多时，外围子镜容差趋于宽松；揭示了子镜尺寸、容差放宽程度与最优波前传感时间之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 本研究为超稳空间望远镜热控设计提供了定量依据，支持未来类地行星探测任务中高对比度成像系统的工程实现。

Abstract: Direct imaging and characterization of Earth-like planets require ultra-stable wavefronts to achieve a starlight suppression level of 100 parts per trillion (ppt) in the coronagraphic dark region of the host star. Thermal drifts in the telescope may affect the wavefront stability. In this context, we present a segment-level thermal stability error budget for segmented space telescopes crucial to Earth-like planet detection and specify requirements for an ultra-stable telescope. Our study utilizes multiple segmented primary mirror architectures, each with their respective apodizer solution developed by the Segmented Coronagraph Design & Analysis research team at the Space Telescope Science Institute, tailored to an off-axis ~6 m-aperture space telescope design. Using a detailed finite element model provided by L3Harris Technologies, we relate the temperature gradient at the location of the primary mirror to wavefront variations on each segment. We allocate both static and dynamic thermal tolerances for each segment using the Pair-based Analytical model for Segmented Telescope Imaging from Space sensitivity approach, and a batch-estimation algorithm respectively. Our analysis shows a non-uniform tolerance allocation across all segments of the primary mirror, as a result of apodization, and generally, with an increase in segment numbers, the tolerances for outer segments become less stringent. We observe trade-offs between segment size, tolerance relaxation and optimal wavefront sensing time to achieve a desired dark-hole contrast.

</details>


### [106] [Statistical treatment of searches for counterparts of positionally-uncertain astrophysical sources: from flux upper limits to detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28473)
*Julian Sitarek,Abelardo Moralejo,Juan Jiménez Quiles,Giacomo D'Amico,Andrea Simongini,Antonio Stamerra*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在多信使和多波段天体物理学中，如何利用不同分辨率仪器的观测数据对弱信号源进行统计定位，比较了频率学、贝叶斯及无先验三种方法的可靠性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多信使和多波段天文观测快速增长，同一事件常被不同角分辨率的仪器观测，需对低显著性源进行统计推断以解释无明确对应体的警报。

Method: 采用基于警报概率密度函数的频率学方法和贝叶斯方法，并对比不使用该信息的无先验（agnostic）方法；通过一维玩具模拟和真实引力波事件伽马射线望远镜后随观测模拟进行评估。

Result: 频率学与贝叶斯方法在弱信号情况下性能相近且均优于无先验方法。

Conclusion: 在弱信号源定位任务中，充分利用警报位置的概率密度信息（无论频率学或贝叶斯框架）可显著提升统计推断可靠性，而忽略该信息的无先验方法效果较差。

Abstract: Rapid growth of the multimessenger and multiwavelength astrophysics had led to an increasing number of observations of the same events with instruments with different point spread functions. In particular pointing instruments with good angular resolution are used to pin-point the source of poorly-localized alert. In case no clear detection of the source counterpart is reached, the interpretation of the results requires statistical analysis. We investigate two approaches using the probability density function of the alert: frequentist and Bayesian, as well as agnostic approach not using this information. We discuss the advantages and problems of all the methods, and compare their reliability and performance. We consider both a simple one-dimensional toy simulations and a realistic use case of full simulations of the follow-up of a gravitational wave event with gamma-ray telescopes. The performance of both frequentist and Bayesian approaches for weak signals is comparable and superior to the agnostic one.

</details>


### [107] [DB-Bench: Benchmarking Deblenders for LSST DESC Using the Blending ToolKit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28475)
*Aidan Berres,Grant Merz,Xin Liu,the LSST Dark Energy Science Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文利用Blending ToolKit (BTK) 对LSST数据中的星系图像解混算法（SourceExtractor、SCARLET、DeepDISC）进行了端到端基准测试，重点评估其在未识别混叠（unrecognized blends）情形下的性能，发现各算法优劣互补：SCARLET在分割与重建上表现最优，DeepDISC对低信噪比源检测召回率高，SourceExtractor峰值定位准但分割与重建能力弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LSST数据中星系混叠将成为下游科学分析（尤其是宇宙学分析）的主要系统误差来源，亟需评估现有解混算法在真实巡天条件下的鲁棒性与局限性。

Method: 基于Blending ToolKit构建可复现、定制化的混叠图像样本，系统测试SourceExtractor、SCARLET和DeepDISC三种主流解混算法；采用标准化指标（检测精度、分割准确率、源重建质量）进行定量评估，并考察源间距、亮度等关键参数的影响。

Result: SCARLET在分割准确率和源重建质量上表现最佳；DeepDISC对暗弱、低信噪比源的检测召回率最高；SourceExtractor虽峰值定位准确，但分割与重建性能较差；所有算法在未识别混叠情形下均存在不同程度的系统偏差。

Conclusion: 当前解混算法各具优势与短板，尚无法全面满足LSST科学需求；本基准测试为算法改进提供了明确方向，强调需提升对未识别混叠的识别与处理能力。

Abstract: Blending will be a major source of systematic uncertainty in downstream science analyses of LSST data. We benchmark the performance of several deblenders, leveraging the Blending ToolKit (BTK) to perform rigorous, end-to-end testing. This benchmark incorporates key deblending algorithms, including SourceExtractor, SCARLET, and DeepDISC, with the goal of comparing their effectiveness in handling blended galaxy images from LSST/Rubin simulations. A key focus is characterizing algorithm performance in the regime of unrecognized blends, where multiple galaxies are misidentified as a single object, as these cases introduce systematic biases that propagate into downstream cosmological analyses for galaxy surveys. By utilizing BTK's ability to create customized, reproducible blends, we systematically test these deblenders against different blending conditions, such as source separation and brightness. The toolkit's standardized evaluation metrics, including detection precision, segmentation accuracy, and source reconstruction, are comprehensive assessments of each algorithm's strengths and limitations. Each deblender has performance caveats that may impact their true performance in real survey conditions. We find that SCARLET has high segmentation and reconstruction performance, whereas DeepDISC has strong detection recall for faint and low-SNR sources, and SourceExtractor has accurate peak finding abilities but low segmentation and reconstruction performance. This benchmark provides valuable insights into the performance of existing deblenders and highlights areas for future development.

</details>


### [108] [Upgrading LBTI/NOMIC with a quadruple annular groove phase mask and GeoSnap detector for imaging nearby, habitable-zone exoplanets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28562)
*Kevin Wagner,Manny Montoya,Steve Ertel,Jarron Leisenring,Pontus Forsberg,Samuel Ronayette,Andre Wong,Mikael Karlsson,Olivier Absil,Denis Defrère,Markus Kasper,Jordan Stone,Dániel Apai,Laird Close,Jamie Dietrich,Ewan Douglas,Jamie Drew,Olivier Durney,Marina Fetisova,Kyran Grattan,Olivier Guyon,Jacob Isbell,Sebastián Jorquera,Petri Karvinen,Markku Kuittinen,Hervé Le Coroller,Jared Males,Brittany Miles,Dillon O'Reilly,Eric Pantin,Sascha P. Quanz,Eckhart Spalding,Vivek Vijayakumar,Zach Werber,S. Pete Worden*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了LBTI/NOMIC望远镜系统的两项关键升级：Q-AGPM相位掩模日冕仪和GeoSnap红外探测器，并验证了其在高对比度干涉成像中的性能提升，支持Breakthrough Watch对邻近类太阳恒星宜居带的深度观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升LBTI/NOMIC在热红外波段（尤其是N波段）对系外行星等微弱伴星的高对比度、高分辨成像能力，以支持对邻近类太阳恒星宜居带的深度探测任务。

Method: 设计并安装四环形沟槽相位掩模（Q-AGPM）日冕仪；更换为13微米截止的Teledyne GeoSnap探测器；结合FFTcam条纹跟踪器开展Fizeau干涉成像与注入/恢复测试。

Result: Q-AGPM实现双光束高效切换；GeoSnap显著提升量子效率、读出线性度与信噪比；干涉模式相较单孔径在0.2–1角秒内对比度提升2–4倍；系统已投入Breakthrough Watch项目运行。

Conclusion: 两项硬件升级与新成像序列协同显著增强了LBTI/NOMIC在小角距下的探测灵敏度与对比度，使其成为当前地面热红外高对比度干涉观测的前沿平台。

Abstract: The Large Binocular Telescope Interferometer (LBTI)'s Nulling-Optimized Mid-Infrared Camera (NOMIC) is among the most capable thermal-infrared imaging systems available for high-contrast, high-angular-resolution astronomical observations. Here we describe two in-progress upgrades to LBTI/NOMIC: (1) the design, fabrication, and installation of a quadruple annular groove phase mask (Q-AGPM) coronagraph, and (2) the installation of a 13 micron-cutoff Teledyne GeoSnap array. The Q-AGPM is the first coronagraph to be installed within NOMIC and one of the first optimized for N-band (~11 micron) observations. It places four annular groove phase masks on a single diamond substrate so that, in the LBTI dual-aperture imaging mode, each of the two telescope beams can be chopped between a pair of masks without loss of observing efficiency. The GeoSnap array will replace NOMIC's original AQUARIUS array, delivering higher quantum efficiency, larger well depth, faster and more linear readout, and freedom from the excess low-frequency noise that requires aggressive chopping. Together these upgrades substantially improve the achievable contrast and sensitivity at small angular separations. We also present a high-contrast Fizeau imaging sequence obtained with LBTI's new FFTCam fringe tracker, which confirms the interferometric gain over a single aperture through injection/recovery tests: relative to an equal-time single aperture exposure, the S/N = 3 contrast is a factor of ~2-4 deeper across 0.2-1 arcsec, spanning the contrast- and background-limited regimes. Finally, we describe the role of the upgraded LBTI/NOMIC instrument within the Breakthrough Watch program at the University of Arizona, which aims to perform the deepest observations yet of the habitable zones of the nearest single Sun-like stars.

</details>


### [109] [Laboratory demonstration of low order wavefront control using light reflected off the vortex coronagraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28603)
*Clarissa R. Do Ó,Kane Sjoberg,Luke Lamitina,Susan Redmond,Dimitri Mawet,Jorge Llop-Sayson,Siria Alicata*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文首次在实验室中实现了基于矢量涡旋日冕仪（VVC）反射光的低阶波前传感与控制（LOWFS）倾角-平移闭环，显著提升了高对比度成像稳定性与对比度性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足Habitable Worlds Observatory（HWO）对类地系外行星成像（对比度达1e-10）的需求，需提升波前控制鲁棒性；利用VVC反射光进行LOWFS可缓解残余波前误差影响。

Method: 在Caltech的HCST实验平台上，基于升级后的CATKit2框架，同步实现相位恢复、电场共轭（EFC）和LOWFS倾角-平移闭环控制，并开展12小时开环/闭环对比实验。

Result: 相位恢复将科学相机波前误差从71.6 nm RMS降至7.9 nm RMS；闭环将<1 Hz PSF漂移抑制超两个数量级，指向稳定性达<0.005 λ/D；与EFC协同运行时，一小时内保持暗区对比度~5e-8，而开环下因0.2–0.4 λ/D漂移导致对比度下降超一个数量级。

Conclusion: VVC反射光可用于可靠、高效的LOWFS，为未来高对比度日冕成像系统的波前控制架构提供了可行技术路径。

Abstract: The Astro2020 Decadal Survey identified exoplanet imaging as a high priority for the Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO), which must image and characterize exo-Earths at contrasts of $1\times10^{-10}$. The vector vortex coronagraph (VVC) is a leading architecture for this task owing to its small inner working angle and high throughput. Using light reflected from the VVC for wavefront sensing and control provides a potential path to improving robustness to residual wavefront errors and minimizing contrast degradation. Here we present the first laboratory demonstration of a low order wavefront sensing and control (LOWFS) tip-tilt loop operating on light reflected from a VVC, carried out on the High Contrast and Spectroscopy Testbed (HCST) at Caltech's Exoplanet Technology Laboratory. The demonstration is enabled by HCST's upgrade to CATKit2, a service-oriented framework in which we implement phase retrieval, electric field conjugation (EFC), and the LOWFS loop as concurrent routines. Our phase retrieval reduces the science camera wavefront error from 71.6 to 7.9 nm RMS, a $>$9$\times$ improvement. Over a 12 hour open loop run, we find that PSF drift is strongly correlated with bench temperature ($r>0.88$). Closing the tip-tilt loop suppresses drift below 1 Hz by more than two orders of magnitude and holds pointing to $<0.005~λ/D$. Run concurrently with EFC, the closed loop maintains a dark hole contrast of $\sim$5$\times10^{-8}$ over one hour, whereas in open loop a drift of $\sim$0.2--0.4 $λ/D$ degrades contrast by more than an order of magnitude. These results establish reflected light sensing off a VVC as a viable foundation for future wavefront control architectures in high contrast coronagraphy.

</details>

### [110] [Hubble tension problem encompassed by phase-space quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27285)
*Alex E. Bernardini*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于Weyl-Wigner量子力学与广义相空间量子宇宙学框架，提出一种解析方法，通过残余量子修正抑制ΛCDM模型中早期与晚期哈勃常数预测间的分歧，从而缓解哈勃张力问题，并可推广至含曲率及暗成分修正的量子宇宙学场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前宇宙学中由早期（CMB）与晚期（本地测量）观测导致的哈勃常数不一致——即哈勃张力问题。

Method: 在minisuperspace框架下构建局域化相空间量子态，利用Weyl-Wigner量子力学导出爱因斯坦-弗里德曼方程的残余量子修正项。

Result: 量子效应能有效抑制哈勃张力在早期与晚期宇宙预测之间的发散；该方法不仅适用于标准ΛCDM模型，还可拓展至含空间曲率和暗能量/暗物质修正的广义量子宇宙学模型。

Conclusion: 相空间量子宇宙学提供了一种自然、解析且可推广的机制来缓解哈勃张力，无需引入新物理成分，仅依赖于量子引力效应的低能有效表现。

Abstract: Analytical solutions encompassing the so-called Hubble tension problem are revisited through the framework of Weyl--Wigner quantum mechanics and discussed in the context of generalized phase-space scenarios of quantum cosmology. After reviewing the nature of the problem and its recent developments, an extended formulation constructed within the quantum phase-space framework to address the Hubble tension is proposed. For the quantum cosmology described in the minisuperspace framework through (generic) localized phase-space quantum states, when residual quantum corrections to the Einstein--Friedmann equation are analytically derived, quantum effects are shown to suppress the Hubble tension divergence between early- and late-time predictions. Besides addressing the Hubble tension problem within the standard $Λ$CDM cosmological model, our approach encompasses generalized quantum cosmological scenarios that also include curvature and dark sector modifications.

</details>


### [111] [Nonlinear Dynamics near the Threshold of Gravitational Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27343)
*Jaime Redondo-Yuste,Josu C. Aurrekoetxea*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论模拟标量场自引力坍缩过程，研究了围绕平直时空的微扰展开（包括次次领头阶效应）的有效性范围，并发现当峰值光度达到约10^{-2}倍普朗克光度时，微扰理论开始失效，出现频率红移和幂律谱等非线性特征，与近似离散自相似性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确理解微扰理论在强引力场中的适用范围至关重要，而标量场自引力坍缩提供了一个干净的实验室来系统研究从微扰到非微扰的过渡。

Method: 采用数值相对论方法模拟不同初始振幅的标量场坍缩，分析其辐射光度、频谱特性等，并对比包含次次领头阶的平直时空微扰预测。

Result: 发现当峰值光度L_{peak} ~ 10^{-2} L_{Planck}时，微扰预测显著偏离数值结果；非线性效应表现为驱动频率红移和幂律谱，且该谱符合近似离散自相似性。

Conclusion: 微扰理论在强场动力学中存在明确的失效阈值，该阈值可通过物理可观测量（如峰值光度）标定；所得非线性特征为理解更现实场景（如非球对称坍缩、高速黑洞并合）中微扰极限提供了关键线索。

Abstract: Perturbation theory is an essential tool to model and interpret gravitational dynamics, for example, binary black hole mergers. Therefore it is also crucial to precisely understand its regimes of validity. The collapse of a scalar field under its own self-gravity provides a clean laboratory to study these questions. By varying the field's initial amplitude we can transition smoothly between a perturbative regime, where the field scatters in an approximately flat spacetime; and a nonperturbative regime, where a black hole forms in finite time. In this work, we use numerical relativity simulations of this set-up to investigate the accuracy of a perturbative expansion around flat spacetime, including next-to-next-to-leading order effects. Our simulations show deviations from these perturbative predictions before black hole formation, once the maximum luminosity of the process is sufficiently large, $L_{peak} \sim 10^{-2} L_{Planck}$. We characterize these nonlinear effects including a redshift of the driving frequency and a power-law spectral amplitude, which we show is consistent with approximate discrete self-similarity. These results provide a step forward towards understanding the limits of perturbative expansions in more realistic strong-gravity phenomena such as non-spherical collapse and high-velocity black hole mergers.

</details>


### [112] [Inflation in unimodular loop quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27344)
*Steffen Gielen,Rita B. Neves*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了幺模圈量子宇宙学中的暴胀模型，采用具有明确几何意义的幺模时间而非传统标量场作为时间参数，推导了有效方程的解析与数值解，并探讨了α-吸引子势下动能或势能主导的反弹模型及其观测兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决标准圈量子宇宙学中依赖自由无质量标量场作为时钟的问题，引入幺模时间以提供与物质内容无关、具清晰几何意义的时间演化框架。

Method: 基于幺模圈量子宇宙学框架，利用半经典有效方程，对常数势、二次势及Starobinsky势等情形进行解析与数值求解，并比较不同初始条件下的动力学行为。

Result: 获得常数势下的解析解；对二次势和Starobinsky势给出数值解；发现α-吸引子势可实现动能或势能主导的量子反弹，且与当前宇宙学观测兼容，可能保留量子引力印记。

Conclusion: 幺模时间提供更自然、几何化的时间演化描述，拓展了圈量子宇宙学对暴胀和早期宇宙建模的能力，尤其在构建可观测的量子引力效应模型方面具有潜力。

Abstract: We study inflation in the setting of unimodular loop quantum cosmology, where time evolution is defined in unimodular time rather than with respect to a free, massless scalar field as is standard in loop quantum cosmology. The unimodular setting leads to a natural Schrödinger time evolution in a time coordinate with clear geometric meaning, defined independently of any particular matter content; an inflaton can be included but is not needed as a clock. We review the unimodular version of loop quantum cosmology and comment on possible connections to full (unimodular) loop quantum gravity. Then, focusing on semiclassical effective equations, we derive analytical solutions in simple cases such as a constant potential, emphasising the use of a unimodular time coordinate. We also discuss numerical solutions for phenomenologically interesting cases such as a quadratic potential and Starobinsky inflation, comparing different possible choices of initial conditions. In particular, we show that choosing an $α$-attractor potential allows for models of a bounce either dominated by kinetic or potential energy, which are compatible with observations while potentially including observable imprints of the quantum-gravity regime.

</details>


### [113] [Gauge vs (hidden) physical symmetries of FLRW cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27351)
*Andrea Calcinari,Adrià Delhom,Federico Greco,Daniele Oriti,Néstor Rivero*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平坦FLRW宇宙学背景下耦合任意n个无质量标量场时，规范对称性与物理对称性（尤其是隐藏的物理对称性）之间的关系；通过最小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量构造出狄拉克可观测量，并发现其泊松代数构成最大共形代数so(n+1,2)；进一步分析Eisenhart-Duval提升在不同规范下的对称性表现，指出Schrödinger代数仅在特定谐波规范下显现，实为规范依赖而非物理对称；去参数化将提升后的荷映射为规范不变可观测量，且仅在谐波规范下才完整复现共形代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广义协变理论中，坐标时间演化属于规范变换，因此规范固定后显现的对称性未必对应真实物理对称性；而去参数化虽消除了规范自由度，却可能掩盖依赖于所选物理时钟的物理对称性。本文旨在厘清最小超空间模型中规范对称性与物理对称性的区别与联系。

Method: 基于平坦FLRW几何与n个自由无质量标量场耦合的模型，利用最小超空间度规的共形Killing矢量构造守恒荷；结合狄拉克可观测量定义验证其规范不变性；系统分析Eisenhart-Duval提升在不同规范（特别是谐波规范）下的对称代数结构；并通过去参数化映射考察其与物理对称性的对应关系。

Result: 共形Killing矢量生成的守恒荷是狄拉克可观测量，其泊松代数为最大共形代数so(n+1,2)；Eisenhart-Duval提升的显式对称代数随规范变化，在谐波规范下扩大为Schrödinger代数（非物理对称）；去参数化所得可观测量始终实现共形代数的子代数，并仅在谐波规范下恢复完整共形代数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套分离规范对称性与物理对称性的框架，不仅恢复了约化相空间描述所遗漏的守恒荷，而且该方法可推广至含势能的情形，为量子引力及宇宙学对称性分析提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: In generally covariant theories evolution in coordinate time is a gauge transformation, so that a symmetry made manifest in a gauge-fixed description need not be a symmetry of the physical dynamics. Deparametrisation, in turn, removes gauge symmetries but may hide physical symmetries, in particular those dependent on the chosen physical clock. We study the relation between gauge and (hidden) physical symmetries in flat FLRW geometry coupled to an arbitrary number $n$ of free massless scalar fields. We show that conformal Killing vectors of the minisuperspace metric generate conserved charges which are Dirac observables--hence gauge-invariant--and whose Poisson algebra is the maximal conformal algebra $\mathfrak{conf}(n,1)\simeq\mathfrak{so}(n+1,2)$, extending previous single-field results to arbitrary $n$. We then revisit the Eisenhart-Duval lift in a family of gauges and show that the manifest symmetry algebra is gauge dependent, enlarging to the Schrödinger algebra (which is thus not a physical symmetry) in the distinguished harmonic gauge where the gauge-fixed minisuperspace metric becomes flat. Further, deparametrisation maps the lifted charges to gauge-invariant Dirac observables, which always realise a subalgebra of the conformal algebra and reproduce it in full in the harmonic gauge. These results establish a framework for separating gauge from physical symmetries in minisuperspace models, recovering charges to which reduced phase-space descriptions are structurally blind, and remaining applicable in the presence of potentials.

</details>


### [114] [Quark Stars in $f(T,\mathcal{T}) $ Gravity: Structure, Stability, and Observational Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27365)
*Takol Tangphati,Ayan Banerjee,Izzet Sakalli,Sayantan Ghosh,Aseel Smerat*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在f(T,T)引力理论下夸克星的结构特性，发现其最大质量随耦合常数变化呈现非单调行为，并在一定正耦合范围内满足两倍太阳质量脉冲星约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 夸克星作为完全由去禁闭夸克物质构成的假想致密天体，为检验广义相对论之外的引力理论提供了理想场所。

Method: 采用标准MIT袋模型描述夸克物质，在f(T,𝒯)引力框架下求解修正的恒星结构方程，分析质量、半径、致密度和表面红移随单个耦合常数变化的行为。

Result: 最大质量随耦合常数非单调变化：先高于广义相对论值，在中等正值耦合下达到约2.02倍太阳质量峰值，随后在接近临界值时急剧下降；两倍太阳质量脉冲星约束在有限正耦合区间内满足；所有候选稳定构型均满足因果律且致密度与表面红移低于广义相对论基准。

Conclusion: f(T,𝒯)引力理论下夸克星结构对耦合参数敏感，存在满足观测约束的可行参数窗口，表明该理论具有可检验性。

Abstract: Quark stars-hypothetical compact stars made entirely of deconfined quark matter-offer a clean testing ground for gravity beyond general relativity. We study their structure in $f(T,\mathcal{T})$ gravity, a teleparallel theory in which torsion is coupled directly to the trace of the energy-momentum tensor through a single constant coupling. Using the standard MIT bag description of quark matter, we solve the modified stellar structure equations and follow how the mass, radius, compactness, and surface redshift respond as the coupling is varied across its full admissible range. The maximum mass turns out to depend on the coupling in a non-monotonic way: it rises above the general relativity value, peaks near 2.02 solar masses at a moderate positive coupling, and then falls steeply as the coupling approaches a critical value at which the structure equations become singular. The two-solar-mass pulsar constraint is satisfied within a finite window of positive couplings. All configurations on the candidate stable branch satisfy causality and remain below the standard general-relativistic compactness and surface-redshift benchmarks.

</details>


### [115] [Structural morphology and the gravitational arrow of time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27526)
*Julian Barbour,Francisco S. N. Lobo,Maria I. R. Lourenço*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了零能量、零线性动量和零角动量约束下的牛顿N体问题，聚焦于尺度不变的聚类对比度量V（称为variety）与局部几何形态（用六邻域各向异性A6表征）之间的关系；发现两个V值相近的临界构型具有显著不同的局部各向异性分布，表明全局标量V不足以唯一确定形态结构，需引入局域关系观测量作为补充描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解引力箭头（BKM arrow）所依赖的全局标量V是否足以刻画系统形态结构；探究尺度不变形变空间中的临界构型能否作为探测结构信息的有效探针。

Method: 基于N=5000的二维数值粒子模拟，选取两个central configuration，计算其全局variety V及局部六邻域各向异性A6；系统比较不同邻域大小（k=4至12）及鲁棒性检验下的A6统计特性。

Result: 尽管两构型V值仅相差1.686%，其平均A6相差156.8%（0.1331 vs 0.3419），且高V构型中18.1%粒子A6>0.5，低V构型中无一满足；该差异在多种邻域尺度和鲁棒性检验下均稳健存在。

Conclusion: 全局标量variety V不能唯一决定局部几何形态，局域关系观测量（如A6）是必要补充；这为连接静态形变空间景观与真实Janus点演化路径的形态学提供了定量桥梁。

Abstract: The Newtonian $N$-body problem in the zero-energy, zero-linear-momentum, and zero-angular-momentum sector provides a time-reversal-invariant setting in which generic complete solutions possess a Janus point and exhibit a gravitational arrow on both branches away from it. The dimensionless variety $V$, a global scale-invariant measure of clustering contrast, is not pointwise monotonic but fluctuates while growing between rising bounds away from the Janus region. Central configurations are critical shapes of the same scale-invariant landscape and therefore provide controlled probes of the structural information encoded by $V$. We investigate this question for two planar $N=5000$ central configurations using the numerical particle-coordinate data. Local morphology is quantified by the six-neighbour anisotropy $A_6$, which ranges from $0$ for an isotropic local environment to $1$ for an effectively one-dimensional one. Although the varieties of the two configurations differ by only $1.686\%$, their mean anisotropies differ by $156.8\%$, from $0.1331$ to $0.3419$. Moreover, $18.1\%$ of the particles in the higher-variety configuration satisfy $A_6>0.5$, whereas none do so in the lower-variety configuration. The anisotropy ordering persists for all tested neighbourhood sizes $4\le k\le12$, and the principal contrast survives a dimensionless close-pair robustness test. For this pair of critical shapes, nearby values of the global variety therefore coexist with markedly different local geometrical organization. Thus the scalar quantity whose long-term behavior characterizes the BKM gravitational arrow does not, by itself, uniquely specify morphology. This identifies local relational observables as a complementary level of description and provides a quantitative bridge between the static shape-space landscape and morphology along genuine Janus-point histories.

</details>


### [116] [Tidal Love numbers of multi-state Boson stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27587)
*Xin-Lei Zhao,Jun-Ru Chen,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了由基态和第一激发态复标量场组成的多态玻色子星（MSBSs）的潮汐Love数，分析了同步与非同步频率条件下的背景解分类，并讨论了不同解的场函数、ADM质量和结合能；结果表明电潮汐Love数先正后负，磁潮汐Love数恒为负且绝对值更小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究多态玻色子星的潮汐响应特性，以深化对致密天体物态方程及引力波信号特征的理解。

Method: 数值求解爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登方程组，分类同步与非同步频率下的背景解（单支/双支），并计算ℓ=2电、磁四极潮汐Love数。

Result: 电潮汐Love数在参数满足μ̃₁ > 0.891 或 ω̃₀ > 0.777 时由正突变为负；磁潮汐Love数始终为负，其绝对值小于电潮汐Love数。

Conclusion: 多态玻色子星的潮汐Love数具有显著参数依赖性和符号跃变行为，可作为区分其物态与单态模型的潜在引力波观测判据。

Abstract: In this paper, we calculate the tidal Love numbers of multi-state boson stars (MSBSs) composed of ground state and first excited state complex scalar fields. Under synchronized and nonsynchronized frequency conditions, the background solutions of MSBSs are classified into single-branch and double-branch types. The field functions, ADM mass, and binding energy of different solutions are discussed. We then calculate the quadrupolar ($\ell=2$) electric and magnetic tidal Love numbers for branches containing stable solutions. Our results show that the electric tidal Love numbers are initially positive and then suddenly transition to negative values. This phenomenon occurs when the parameters satisfy $\tildeμ_1 > 0.891$ or $\tildeω_0 > 0.777$; for smaller values of these parameters, the electric Love numbers remain positive. The magnetic tidal Love numbers are always negative, with absolute values smaller than those of the electric tidal Love numbers.

</details>


### [117] [Constrained thermodynamics and geodesic observables of an effective non-commutative Kerr-like black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27707)
*H. Hassanabadi,L. A. López,N. Bretón,L. M. Nieto,S. Zare*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非对易背景下一种有效Kerr类黑洞的视界结构、约束热力学及测地线性质，发现非对易变形通过质量依赖的类电荷项改变径向几何，同时保持测地线方程可分离性；分析了视界存在条件、极值零温构型、静止极限面与能层；区分了几何霍金量与固定非对易参数下的共轭热力学变量，推导了正则与巨正则系综热容；计算了球形光子区、赤道光环与阴影边界，显示变形使光子轨道内移、阴影缩小；还研究了类时圆轨道（ISCO），发现非对易修正使顺行与逆行轨道均向内偏移；最后给出不变光子频率偏移，考虑发射体轨道方向与光子切向发射方向的独立选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非对易时空背景下黑洞物理的基本性质，特别是其几何结构、热力学行为与观测特征如何受量子引力效应（以非对易参数表征）影响。

Method: 理论建模与解析计算：构建非对易背景下的有效Kerr类度规；系统分析视界条件、能层与测地线可分离性；基于约束热力学框架区分几何量与热力学共轭变量；推导不同系综下的热容；求解光子球、光环、阴影及ISCO；计算不变频率偏移。

Result: 非对易变形引入质量依赖的类电荷项，改变径向几何但保持测地线可分离；存在新的极值零温黑洞构型；热力学中几何霍金温度与共轭熵等需在固定非对易参数下重新定义；正则与巨正则热容表现出不同局域稳定性；光子轨道内移、阴影缩小；ISCO顺行与逆行轨道均内移；频率偏移依赖于轨道与发射方向的独立选择。

Conclusion: 非对易变形显著修改Kerr黑洞的关键物理特征，既保留部分经典结构（如测地线可分离），又引入新热力学与观测效应，为检验量子引力低能效应提供了可观测的理论预言。

Abstract: We investigate the horizon structure, constrained thermodynamics, and geodesic properties of an effective Kerr-like black hole in a non-commutative background. Deformation modifies the radial geometry through a mass-dependent charge-like contribution, while preserving the separability of the geodesic equations. We determine the conditions for horizon existence, identify the extremal zero-temperature configuration, and analyze the stationary-limit surfaces and the ergoregion. Special attention is paid to the thermodynamic interpretation of the model, where the geometric Hawking quantities are distinguished from the conjugate variables associated with the constrained state space at fixed non-commutative deformation parameter. The canonical and grand-canonical heat capacities are derived to characterize their ensemble-dependent local thermal behavior. We also obtain the spherical photon region, equatorial light rings, and shadow boundary, showing that the deformation shifts the characteristic photon orbits inwards and reduces the overall size of the shadow. Timelike circular motion is studied through the innermost stable circular orbit, where non-commutative correction produces an inward shift of both the prograde and retrograde branches. Finally, invariant photon frequency shifts are obtained by treating the emitter's orbital direction and the photon's tangential emission direction as independent physical choices.

</details>


### [118] [Degenerate Limits of Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27801)
*Bekir Baytaş,Xiao-Kan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究标量-张量引力理论在连接动力学框架下的退化极限，通过局部缩放变换实现空间三重态的退化，并推导出退化标量-张量引力的约束条件及其物理意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索标量-张量引力理论在退化极限下的行为，以理解其在极端几何条件下的理论结构与物理内涵。

Method: 采用哈密顿连接动力学形式，通过对空间三重态实施局部缩放变换，系统推导退化极限下各几何量及关系的变化，并导出相应的约束方程。

Result: 得到了退化标量-张量引力理论的完整约束体系，并分析了这些约束所蕴含的物理含义。

Conclusion: 退化极限下的标量-张量理论仍保持自洽的动力学结构，其约束条件揭示了该理论在奇异几何背景下的潜在物理性质。

Abstract: We study the degenerate limits of the scalar-tensor theory of gravity in its Hamiltonian connection dynamics formulation. The degenerate limit of the spatial triad is effectuated by a local scaling transformation of the triad, which leads to the degenerate limits of all other geometric quantities and relations in the connection dynamics. We derive the constraints for the degenerate scalar-tensor theory of gravity and discuss their physical implications.

</details>


### [119] [Foldy--Wouthuysen Transformation of the Generalized Dirac Equation in Symmetric Teleparallel Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27889)
*Sabutay Ugur,Muzaffer Adak,Ali Bagci,Caglar Pala*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在对称度规仿射引力背景下广义狄拉克方程的非相对论极限，通过Foldy-Wouthuysen变换导出了包含新型自旋-引力耦合项的有效哈密顿量，并讨论其在地球弱引力场中的可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对称度规仿射引力（symmetric teleparallel gravity）中广义狄拉克方程的低能行为，拓展标准狄拉克理论对非度规性（non-metricity）的响应，寻找新自旋-引力相互作用通道。

Method: 在协变规范和弱场Schwarzschild度规（各向同性坐标）下，构建含完整Clifford代数基的广义旋量联络；推导广义狄拉克哈密顿量，并进行至1/m²阶的Foldy–Wouthuysen变换，保留引力势及其空间导数的一阶项。

Result: 得到块对角哈密顿量，除传统引力类比动能、自旋-轨道、Darwin项外，新增直接自旋-引力、各向异性自旋-动量-引力及潮汐自旋-动量耦合项；并给出地球场中电子的量级估计以验证展开截断合理性。

Conclusion: 广义狄拉克方程在对称度规仿射引力中自然诱导出新型低能自旋-动量-引力耦合，为实验探测非度规性提供有效哈密顿框架与可观测信号。

Abstract: We investigate the non-relativistic limit of the generalized Dirac equation in a weak, static, and spherically symmetric background of symmetric teleparallel gravity. The underlying generalized spinor connection incorporates the complete Clifford-algebra basis and introduces additional couplings to the non-metricity sector beyond those of the conventional Dirac theory. Working in the coincident gauge and adopting the weak-field Schwarzschild geometry in isotropic coordinates, we derive the corresponding generalized Dirac Hamiltonian and perform successive Foldy--Wouthuysen transformations up to order $1/m^2$, retaining terms to first order in the gravitational potential and its spatial derivatives. The resulting block-diagonal Hamiltonian contains not only the expected gravitational counterparts of the kinetic, spin--orbit, and Darwin interactions, but also additional operator structures generated by the generalized spinor connection. In particular, direct spin--gravity, anisotropic spin--momentum--gravity, and tidal spin--momentum couplings arise naturally from the generalized metric-affine interaction. We further perform an order-of-magnitude analysis for an electron in the Earth's weak gravitational field to justify the adopted truncation of the inverse-mass expansion. These results demonstrate that the generalized Dirac equation in a symmetric teleparallel background gives rise to new low-energy interaction channels involving the fermion spin, momentum, and spatial derivatives of the gravitational field. The resulting effective Hamiltonian provides a framework for exploring phenomenological constraints on the additional couplings entering the generalized spinor connection.

</details>


### [120] [Towards long and accurate numerical relativity waveforms of binary black holes beyond general relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27991)
*Guillermo Lara,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Nils Deppe,Lawrence E. Kidder,Geoffrey Lovelace,Sizheng Ma,Alexandra Macedo,Jordan Moxon,Kyle C. Nelli,Mark A. Scheel,William Throwe,Nils L. Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文展示了结合谱方法和“固定方程”方法，成功生成了目前文献中最长的超越广义相对论（GR）理论的数值相对论波形，聚焦于平移对称标量高斯-博内引力理论，并验证其引力波相位修正可与GR区分，导致更早并合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为持续发展利用引力波检验引力理论，需发展超越广义相对论的数值相对论模拟方法。

Method: 采用谱方法与“fixing-the-equations”方法，针对平移对称标量高斯-博内引力理论，模拟等质量、非自旋、低偏心率黑洞双星系统，并在将来零无穷远提取引力波与标量波形。

Result: 获得超过40个引力波周期（20多个轨道）的波形，相位误差小于1弧度；发现该理论中引力波相位修正可与广义相对论区分，且并合时间早于GR。

Conclusion: 所获波形是未来与后牛顿理论精确对比及校准超越GR波形模型的关键基础。

Abstract: Numerical relativity (NR) simulations of compact binaries in theories beyond general relativity (GR) will be pivotal for the continued development of future tests of gravity with gravitational waves (GWs). In this Letter, we show that the combination of spectral methods and the "fixing-the-equations" approach allows us to produce the longest waveforms in the literature for a genuine beyond-GR theory, thus bringing NR methods for alternative theories of gravity closer to the state-of-the-art in GR. For concreteness, we focus on the well-known shift-symmetric version of scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity, a theory postulating the existence of an additional dynamical scalar and describing black holes (BHs) different from the Kerr solution. We extract the gravitational and scalar waveforms at future null infinity for equal-mass, nonspinning, eccentricity-reduced BH binaries, and quantify the phase errors to be $\lesssim$ 1 rad after 40+ GW cycles (20+ orbits). We also show that the GW phase corrections in this alternative theory are distinguishable from Einstein's theory and lead to an earlier coalescence time than in GR. Obtaining such waveforms is a stepping stone to perform precise comparisons with Post-Newtonian theory and to calibrate waveform models beyond GR.

</details>


### [121] [High-accuracy drivers to simulate black hole binaries beyond general relativity with the fixing-the-equations approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28003)
*Guillermo Lara,Harald P. Pfeiffer,Nils Deppe,Lawrence E. Kidder,Geoffrey Lovelace,Sizheng Ma,Alexandra Macedo,Jordan Moxon,Kyle C. Nelli,Mark A. Scheel,William Throwe,Nils L. Vu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在spectre数值相对论代码中实现了“fixing-the-equations”方法，结合伪谱间断伽辽金格式，生成了标量高斯-博内（sGB）引力理论下长时高精度的引力波波形；提出了新的共动驱动方程族以利用准圆双星近似对称性，并解决了张量场驱动中的数值不稳定性问题（如黑洞自旋虚假增长），最终获得相位误差小于1弧度、涵盖近40个引力波周期的高质量波形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在标量高斯-博内引力理论中生成长期稳定、高精度的数值相对论引力波波形，需解决标准演化方案中因约束违反和坐标漂移导致的数值不稳定性问题。

Method: 在spectre中实现'fixing-the-equations'方法；提出适用于准圆双星系统的新型共动驱动方程族；针对张量场设计恰当的驱动推广形式（避免将张量分量简单视为标量）；结合先进偏心率抑制与Cauchy特征演化波提取技术。

Result: 成功验证单黑洞解与解析预测一致；双黑洞早期旋进阶段黑洞本征量对驱动时间尺度不敏感；新张量驱动方案消除了自旋虚假增长；获得偏心率≲10⁻³、相位误差≲1 rad、含记忆效应的近40周期高精度波形。

Conclusion: 所提共动驱动方案显著提升了sGB引力中长期数值模拟的稳定性与精度，为强场检验替代引力理论提供了可靠波形模板。

Abstract: We implement the "fixing-the-equations" approach [Phys.Rev.D 96 (2017) 8, 084043] in spectre, an NR code using a pseudo-spectral discontinuous Galerkin scheme, to produce long and accurate NR waveforms in the well-known shift-symmetric version of scalar Gauss-Bonnet (sGB) gravity. To achieve this, we introduce a new family of comoving driver equations that exploits the approximate symmetries of quasicircular binary systems and is designed to recover the exact (quasi-)stationary solutions of the fully-coupled theory. We validate our single black hole (BH) solutions against analytic predictions and show that, even for binary BHs in the early inspiral, the intrinsic BH quantities are relatively insensitive to the timescales entering the driver equation. Attention is given to the prescription of driver equations for tensors, for which we give an example of how treating tensor components as scalars can lead to undesired behaviour over long timescales, including spurious growth of the BH spins. A more appropriate generalization to the tensor case is given for the comoving driver, which is shown to avoid these issues. Overall, our implementation leverages state-of-the-art methods for eccentricity reduction and wave extraction with Cauchy Characteristic Evolution to simulate systems with eccentricity $\lesssim 10^{-3}$. We obtain waveforms with phase errors $\lesssim \mathcal{O}(1) \, \mathrm{rad}$ over almost 40 GW-cycles, which naturally incorporate memory contributions.

</details>


### [122] [Quartic Scalar Clouds on Fixed Kerr Backgrounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28044)
*Hendrik Mennenga*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在固定Kerr和Schwarzschild时空背景下，具有无界标量势的复标量场的定态非线性云；发现了满足同步条件ω=mΩ_H的有限振幅Q-云，并分析了其在(Ω_H, M_K)平面上的存在区域及向线性云和静态Schwarzschild云的极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义相对论中非线性标量场在旋转黑洞（Kerr）和非旋转黑洞（Schwarzschild）背景下的稳定束缚态结构，特别是Q-clouds的存在性与性质。

Method: 基于固定背景时空（Kerr/Schwarzschild度规），求解非线性复标量场方程，结合同步条件ω=mΩ_H，数值/解析分析Q-clouds的存在域、振幅依赖性和极限行为（如线性化极限、Ω_H→0极限）。

Result: 发现Kerr背景下的二维存在区域；Q-clouds在振幅趋于零时退化为线性云；当Ω_H→0时，同步频率与Noether荷消失，过渡为Schwarzschild背景上的静态非线性标量云；并给出归一化余弦型（类轴子）势下的存在域。

Conclusion: 非线性标量云在Kerr和Schwarzschild时空中均存在，其结构受同步条件与势函数形式显著影响，揭示了黑洞-标量场系统中丰富的非线性束缚态相空间。

Abstract: We study stationary nonlinear clouds of a complex scalar field on fixed Kerr and Schwarzschild backgrounds, with an unbounded scalar potential. On Kerr backgrounds we find finite-amplitude Q-clouds satisfying the synchronisation condition $ω=mΩ_H$. They occupy two-dimensional regions of the ($Ω_H,M_K$) plane and reduce to the corresponding linear clouds as the scalar field amplitude tends to zero. The quartic domain can continue to $Ω_H=0$, where both the synchronised frequency and the Noether charge vanish and the solutions become static nonlinear scalar clouds on a fixed Schwarzschild background. We also compute the existence domain for a normalised, axion-inspired cosine potential.

</details>


### [123] [Signatures of kinetic gravity braiding in cosmological probes of the gravitational field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28207)
*Ahmad Nouri-Zonoz,Farbod Hassani,Julian Adamek,Emilio Bellini,Martin Kunz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了动能引力编织（KGB）模型在相对论宇宙学观测中的特征信号，利用KGB-evolution代码生成光锥数据，并计算了弱引力透镜、Shapiro时延、ISW-RS效应和引力红移等可观测量；结果表明KGB模型通过标量场与度规的导数耦合显著修改引力势的振幅与时演化，在ISW-RS和弱透镜等信号中产生百分之几到几十的尺度依赖偏差，非线性效应在小尺度上不可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验修正引力理论（特别是KGB模型）在宇宙学观测中的可区分性，弥补线性理论在小尺度预测上的不足。

Method: 使用相对论N体模拟代码KGB-evolution生成过去光锥输出，计算多种直接探针引力场的可观测量（弱透镜收敛、Shapiro时延、ISW-RS、引力红移），构建全天球图与角功率谱，并与k-essence模型及线性微扰理论对比。

Result: KGB模型导致引力势振幅和时演化发生尺度依赖改变；ISW-RS信号响应最强（线性区被抑制，非线性区反转并达数十个百分点偏差）；弱透镜收敛在ℓ~10²–10³处比k-essence增强约10–12%；线性理论仅适用于大尺度，小尺度必须计入非线性效应。

Conclusion: KGB模型可通过ISW-RS和弱透镜等相对论性观测有效区分；非线性模拟对精确理论预言至关重要，尤其在中小尺度上。

Abstract: We study the observational signatures of kinetic gravity braiding (KGB) models in relativistic cosmological probes constructed along the past light cone. Using the relativistic $N$-body code KGB-evolution, we generate light-cone outputs and compute several observables that directly probe the gravitational field, including weak gravitational lensing convergence, Shapiro time delay, the integrated Sachs-Wolfe and Rees-Sciama (ISW-RS) effects, and gravitational redshift. Full-sky maps and angular power spectra of these quantities are constructed and compared with $k$-essence models and predictions from linear perturbation theory. We find that the derivative coupling between the scalar field and the metric modifies both the amplitude and the time evolution of the gravitational potentials, producing scale-dependent deviations ranging from a few percent to tens of percent. In particular, the ISW-RS signal exhibits the largest fractional response, as the slower decay of the Weyl potential suppresses the KGB signal in the ISW-dominated regime, whereas nonlinear evolution reverses this trend at higher multipoles, producing differences of tens of percent relative to $k$-essence. Weak gravitational lensing also provides a strong complementary probe and, for the model considered here, exhibits clear deviations from the $k$-essence prediction at small scales with enhancements up to $\sim 10$-$12\%$ at multipoles $\ell \sim 10^2$-$10^3$. Our results show that linear perturbation theory accurately describes the large-scale behaviour, while nonlinear effects become important at smaller scales, particularly for the ISW-RS signal and, more moderately, for the convergence, and must therefore be included for reliable theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [124] [Homogeneous and Isotropic Linearized Gravity as a Caldeira--Leggett System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28209)
*Pelayo V. Calzada,Pedro Bargueño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文建立了线性化引力与Caldeira-Leggett开放系统模型之间的等价性，将引力纳入系统-环境框架，并导出含谱密度的广义Langevin方程，进而实现度规扰动的约化量子化和随机引力动力学分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将引力理论与成熟的开放系统形式主义（如Caldeira-Leggett模型）相联系，为引力的量子化和随机动力学提供自然框架。

Method: 通过建立线性化引力与Caldeira-Leggett模型的等价性，利用系统-环境消去法导出广义Langevin方程，并采用Laplace方法分析动力学行为。

Result: 得到描述度规扰动的广义Langevin方程，其中环境影响由谱密度刻画；实现了扰动的约化量子化，并发展了随机引力动力学分析。

Conclusion: 线性化引力可被自然地理解为开放量子系统，该等价性为引力的量子退相干、随机演化及有效耗散描述提供了新路径。

Abstract: We establish the equivalence between homogeneous and isotropic linearized gravity with minimally coupled degrees of freedom and the Caldeira--Leggett model of open systems. This connects gravitation to an established system-plus-reservoir formalism, which we use throughout. Eliminating the reservoir reduces the equations of linearized gravity to a generalized Langevin equation for the metric perturbation, with the environmental influence condensed in the spectral density. The equivalence also yields a natural setting for reduced quantization of the perturbation and stochastic gravitational dynamics. We analyze the resulting dynamics with Laplace methods, extending results presented in other open-system settings.

</details>


### [125] [Logarithmic corrections to black hole entropy from minimum-assumptions discretization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28249)
*Pelayo V. Calzada,Ana Alonso-Serrano,Ernesto Contreras,Pedro Bargueño*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在无特定假设下唯一导出熵面积律及固定对数修正项的通用模型，基于普朗克尺度单元的统计组合计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在在最少且无特定模型假设的前提下，统一解释黑洞熵的面积律及其对数修正项。

Method: 将视界离散化为普朗克尺度的不可区分几何单元，通过纯统计组合计数推导熵表达式。

Result: 成功唯一导出熵的面积律，并自然得到一个固定的对数修正项。

Conclusion: 熵的面积律及其对数修正可仅由基本统计原理和几何离散化得出，无需依赖具体量子引力模型。

Abstract: We introduce here a general model, under agnostic and minimum assumptions, to uniquely find the entropy area law with a corresponding fixed logarithmic correction term. In this approach, the horizon is discretized into generic Planck-scale cells representing coarse-grained indistinguishable geometric structures, and it is just the statistical combinatorial counting that determines the form of the entropy terms. We highlight the role of the assumptions and their comparison with known models providing fixed logarithmic contributions to the entropy.

</details>


### [126] [Ab Initio Real-Time Gravitational-Wave Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28265)
*David Yallup,Metha Prathaban,James Alvey,Thomas C. K. Ng,Thibeau Wouters,Nikhil Sarin,Will Handley*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专为GPU设计的嵌套采样核，用于快速引力波参数估计，结合Slice-within-Gibbs方法，在单GPU上对三探测器网络全数据实现中位12分钟的后验推断，四GPU并行缩短至5分钟；采用异步混频压缩数据后，中位耗时降至89秒，可在约2分钟内完成GW170817的进动自旋-潮汐波形推断，推动基于全物理波形的随机采样向实时引力波参数估计迈进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动引力波参数估计向实时化发展，克服传统随机采样方法在计算效率上的瓶颈，尤其针对长时标双中子星信号的高精度、全数据后验推断需求。

Method: 设计GPU原生嵌套采样核，基于Slice-within-Gibbs（SwiG）结构提升混合效率，并结合异步混频（heterodyning）进行数据压缩，支持全物理波形计算与无信息先验下的采样。

Result: 在单GPU上对典型双中子星信号实现中位12分钟全数据推断（三探测器），四GPU并行缩至5分钟；经异步混频压缩后，中位耗时降至89秒，GW170817的进动自旋-潮汐波形推断约需2分钟。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提升了随机采样技术在GPU上的性能极限，使基于全物理波形、从无信息先验出发的引力波参数估计接近实时水平，为未来高频引力波事件响应提供新路径。

Abstract: We present a specialised GPU-native nested sampling kernel targeting rapid parameter estimation for gravitational wave inference problems. Building upon a Slice-within-Gibbs (SwiG) structure for rapid mixing, we investigate how far we can push baseline stochastic sampling techniques on modern GPU hardware. We demonstrate that for typical long-duration binary neutron star signals observed by the LIGO and Virgo detectors, we can achieve well calibrated posterior inference on the full uncompressed data of a three detector network in a median of twelve minutes on a single GPU. This falls to five minutes when sharded across four devices. Utilising heterodyning to compress the data reduces the median wall time across an injection campaign to 89 seconds -- less than the length of the segment itself -- and enables inference with precessing spin, tidal waveforms on GW170817 in around two minutes. This pushes stochastic sampling techniques using full physical waveform calculations, launched from an uninformed prior state, towards real-time gravitational wave parameter estimation.

</details>


### [127] [Post-Newtonian Roche-Lobe-Overflow Prescription for Compact Binary Mass Transfer and the Corresponding Gravitational Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28315)
*Shuai Zhang,Jie Yang,Zi-Han Zhang,Shenghua Yu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了首阶后牛顿近似下的Roche势及Roche瓣溢流质量转移模型，并研究其对致密双星动力学演化与引力波信号的影响，发现质量转移会导致长时间观测中出现显著的相位漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质量转移在双星系统演化及引力波观测中起关键作用，但现有模型多限于牛顿近似，缺乏后牛顿修正。

Method: 基于共转系中的首阶后牛顿流体动力学方程，推导后牛顿Roche势与质量转移公式，并将组分质量时变性纳入双星动力学，计算对运动方程、引力波能量/角动量辐射及远区极化波形的修正。

Result: 质量转移显著影响致密双星的动力学演化；对引力波观测的主要效应是长期累积的 secular 相位漂移。

Conclusion: 引入后牛顿质量转移模型对精确建模超紧凑双星演化及引力波波形至关重要，尤其对长时观测数据的分析具有实际意义。

Abstract: Mass transfer in binary systems is central to many astrophysical phenomena, including the evolution of compact interacting binaries. Starting from the first post-Newtonian hydrodynamic equations in the corotating frame, we derive the first post-Newtonian Roche potential and construct the corresponding post-Newtonian form of the Roche lobe overflow mass transfer prescription. We then include the time dependence of the component masses in the binary dynamics and compute the associated corrections to the equations of motion, gravitational-wave energy and angular-momentum fluxes, and far-zone polarization waveforms. Finally, we apply the model to representative ultracompact binary systems. We find that mass transfer can play an important role in the dynamical evolution of compact binaries. For gravitational-wave observations, its main effect appears as a secular phase drift accumulated over long observation times.

</details>


### [128] [The BGV Theorem and the Null Convergence Condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28412)
*William H. Kinney*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了零收敛条件（NCC）与Borde-Guth-Vilenkin（BGV）定理之间的关系，指出在特定条件下（正交类时测地线丛、无剪切与涡旋、非正空间曲率）NCC可推出BGV定理；但在存在剪切或非测地叶状结构时，BGV定理不再仅由NCC和曲率保证，并给出了适用于渐近过去dS型永恒暴胀的推广BGV条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清NCC与BGV定理之间的逻辑关系，尤其在更一般时空几何（含剪切、非测地性）下检验BGV定理的适用边界，并为永恒暴胀模型提供更稳健的奇点性结论基础。

Method: 基于广义相对论中的Raychaudhuri方程与时空叶状结构分析，推导局部膨胀率在不同几何条件（剪切、加速度、曲率）下的表达式，比较NCC约束与BGV原始假设的等价性与差异。

Result: 证明在无剪切、无涡旋、正交测地叶状且³ℛ ≤ 0时，NCC蕴含BGV结论；但在含剪切或非测地情况下，NCC + ³ℛ ≤ 0不足以保证BGV定理成立；提出适用于渐近过去dS暴胀的推广BGV条件。

Conclusion: NCC并非BGV定理的充分条件——其有效性高度依赖于时空的具体动力学与几何结构；对永恒暴胀等实际宇宙学模型，需采用更精细的膨胀判据而非简单套用原始BGV假设。

Abstract: We examine the relationship between the Null Convergence Condition (NCC) and the Borde-Guth-Vilenkin (BGV) Theorem. We first show that, for an expanding spacetime foliated orthogonally to a timelike geodesic congruence with vanishing shear and vorticity, the BGV Theorem follows when the NCC holds and the spatial curvature is non-positive, ${}^3\mathcal{R} \leq 0$. The situation becomes more complex in the presence of shear, or non-geodesic threading of the spacetime. In these cases, the local expansion that enters the BGV construction depends not only on the local scalar expansion, but acquires terms given by the contraction of the shear and acceleration with locally defined spatial unit vectors. In the general case, null convergence and non-positive curvature are no longer sufficient to guarantee that the BGV Theorem holds. We discuss the result in the context of eternal inflation in the presence of comoving curvature perturbations and state a more general version of the BGV condition relevant to asymptotically past-de Sitter eternal inflation.

</details>


### [129] [Dynamics of compact binary systems in massive scalar Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28448)
*Iris van Gemeren,Tanja Hinderer,Stefan Vandoren*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了带有质量标量场的标量-高斯-博内特（sGB）引力理论中非自转黑洞双星系统的后牛顿动力学，首次在1PN阶上包含了标量质量与高曲率耦合的修正，并分析了其对束缚能和引力波的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索广义相对论在强场下的可能高曲率修正，特别是引入标量场质量后带来的新物理现象。

Method: 采用后牛顿（PN）近似方法，计算了非自转黑洞双星系统在质量sGB理论中的运动方程、质心变换和圆轨道与偏心轨道的束缚能，至1PN阶，并对小标量质量比做二次展开。

Result: 标量质量修正对规范不变束缚能贡献同号与异号项，整体呈负向修正、略微降低束缚能；效应在大质量比和高偏心率系统中最强。

Conclusion: 该工作为后续计算此类系统产生的引力波波形提供了理论基础，揭示了标量质量在强场引力探测中的可观测效应。

Abstract: Inspiraling binary systems of compact objects probe gravity in strong-field regimes, thereby exploring potential higher curvature corrections to General Relativity. Parity-invariant quadratic corrections can be described by scalar-Gauss-Bonnet (sGB) theory, which involves a scalar field dynamically coupled to curvature scalars and can give rise to scalar condensates around black holes. Considering a mass for the scalar field is natural and leads to new phenomenology related to this additional scale. We compute the dynamics of a binary system of nonspinning black holes in massive sGB using the post-Newtonian (PN) approximation. We obtain solutions for the equations of motion, center-of-mass transformation, and binding energy for circular and eccentric orbits up to 1PN order, where for the first time the higher curvature coupled to scalar mass corrections are included. While most of our calculations are valid for generic scalar masses, the final explicit expressions assume that the mass is small compared to the total mass of the binary and expand to quadratic order in this ratio. We show that the scalar mass corrections to the gauge-invariant binding energy come with same and opposite sign order terms, contributing an overall opposite sign contribution in the perturbative limit, decreasing the binding energy slightly. The effects are largest for binary systems with high mass ratio and large eccentricity. Our methods and results will also be useful as a basis for computing the gravitational waves sourced by such systems.

</details>
